papers/30-free-monoid-incidence-indistinguishability/NARRATIVE_REPORT.md:7:The decisive fact is not a bad coefficient choice.  Positive-integer divisibility is itself a free commutative monoid.  Valuation vectors identify it with formal monomials, preserving the pointed order, atoms, lcms, intervals, and Möbius values. papers/30-free-monoid-incidence-indistinguishability/NARRATIVE_REPORT.md:15:The Paper28/29 analytic stack remains useful for bookkeeping.  Weighting its mixed Gram series by the Boolean pair kernel gives a normally convergent holomorphic function and an exact ratio-frequency ledger.  That series is a new functional, not an ordinary trace or a modified Fredholm determinant.  A separately defined absolutely summable zero-diagonal Gram matrix is trace class and owns an honest auxiliary ordinary Fredholm determinant.  Its phase cancels, its coefficients are cloned, and it does not upgrade the original chiral determinant.
papers/30-free-monoid-incidence-indistinguishability/NARRATIVE_REPORT.md:19:The local counterterm analysis of incidence-based symbolic dynamics leaves a possible nonlocal escape: a source-natural invariant built from several atoms, their joins, incidence Möbius values, or connected cumulants.  We show that this escape is unavailable for the admitted multiplicative source.  Unique factorization identifies positive-integer divisibility with the free commutative monoid of finitely supported valuation vectors.  After compatible cutoffs and all roof, Gram, and compiler decorations are transported, this map is an isomorphism of decorated sources.  Hence every isomorphism-natural local or nonlocal invariant agrees on the integer source and its formal UFD clone.  It is impossible to be nonzero on the former while vanishing on every UFD control.  The Boolean weight is one on both sources; its partition-lattice cumulant is zero on the factorizing baseline.  A stronger triple filter is nonzero at three integer cutoffs and zero on four finite non-UFD fixtures, but free and polynomial-UFD clones copy its full ledger.  The final audit passes 28/28 tests and 1616/1616 checks.  The mixed Gram series is holomorphic for \(1-2\eta<\operatorname{Re}s<2\eta\) but remains a new functional.  A separate trace-class Gram matrix owns an auxiliary ordinary determinant; neither object upgrades the original chiral determinant, and both are cloned.  The multiplicative incidence/counterterm branch is therefore closed.
papers/30-free-monoid-incidence-indistinguishability/NARRATIVE_REPORT.md:25:关联型符号动力学的局部反项分析仍留下一个非局部缺口：是否可由多个原子、其并、关联 Möbius 函数或连通累积量构造源自然不变量。本文证明，在既定乘法源边界内该缺口不存在。唯一分解把正整数整除源同构到有限支撑赋值向量组成的自由交换幺半群；将相容截断以及屋顶权、Gram 核和编译器装饰沿此映射搬运后，二者成为完整装饰源的同构。因此，任意在同构下自然的局部或非局部不变量都无法在整数基线非零而在所有 UFD 控制上为零。更强的三元一致性过滤器在三个整数截断上非零并在四个有限非 UFD 样例上精确为零，但自由交换与多项式 UFD 克隆逐项复制其账本；最终审计通过 28/28 测试与 1616/1616 独立检查。混合 Gram 级数是全纯新泛函，而另行构造的迹类 Gram 矩阵拥有一个辅助普通行列式；二者都不能升级原手性行列式且都被克隆。该结果正式关闭旧的乘法手性关联反项分支。

docs/prior_work/legacy/5-riemann_henon/paper_npj/main.tex:57:Given this, our motivation is not to claim that RMT ``fails.'' Rather, we ask a constructive and complementary question: \emph{can a single, low-dimensional deterministic dynamical system generate, within one model and without a manual unfolding step, both the global mean density and the local repulsion of the zeros?} A positive numerical answer would not contradict RMT; it would offer a concrete dynamical object whose spectrum exhibits both scales simultaneously, in the spirit of the Hilbert--P\'olya program.

docs/prior_work/legacy/4-riemann_logistic/paper/fracfract/main.tex:40:\abstract{Finding a dynamical system whose spectrum reproduces the non-trivial zeros of the Riemann $\zeta$ function is a long-standing question in mathematical physics. Building on our recent result that the symbolic dynamics of the logistic map at its band-merging point is isomorphic to the prime sieve \cite{wang2026}, we study a discrete dynamical operator driven by a non-autonomous logarithmic cooling schedule ($\mu_n \sim 1/\ln^2 n$) and compare its spectrum numerically to the Riemann zeros. At low and intermediate orders the model reproduces the first zeros with small error and exhibits a change of behaviour near $N\approx 20$; we note a qualitative spatial coincidence between this numerical feature and the enlarged error bars reported near comparable orders in recent ion-trap quantum simulations, but we treat this as a heuristic observation rather than evidence of a shared physical mechanism. Extending the evolution to the large-$N$ regime ($N\ge 1000$), an ablation comparison shows that a globally scaled Gaussian Unitary Ensemble surrogate cannot match the logarithmically varying mean density, whereas restoring the conjugate ($\pm\theta$) spectrum drives the fitted ground-state intercept to approximately zero. We present these results as numerical observations and heuristic arguments rather than proofs, and we state explicitly which claims are established, numerical, or conjectural. This offers a non-autonomous, dissipative-dynamics perspective on the Hilbert--P\'olya program.}

papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage3/ANCHORED_REVIEW_DRAFT.md:12: \alpha x_g+\beta x_{ga}+\gamma x_{gb}=0.\] The underlying Heisenberg principal-action framework is established. In particular, \[@GollSchmidtVerbitskiy2014\] develop expansiveness and homoclinic methods for principal actions, and the survey of \[@LindSchmidt2015\] treats the exact integer element \(1+a+b\) as a mixing example. Our object is its zero-dimensional coefficient reduction, not a claim that the group, action framework, or three-term element is new. The question here is narrower and arithmetic: what is the exact kernel dimension after imposing a prime congruence period?
papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage3/ANCHORED_REVIEW_DRAFT.md:31:Finite Heisenberg representation theory, including the Stone–von Neumann description behind the nonlinear blocks, is standard; we use the formulation of \[@GurevichHadani2010\] and the direct finite-group classification of \[@GrassbergerHormann2001\]. The progress here is the resulting symbolic fixed-space phase diagram for the weighted cross-characteristic family. No priority language is intended: the literature search supporting this internal draft is bounded.
papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage3/ANCHORED_REVIEW_DRAFT.md:130:\[rem:left-right\] Our finite operator is right translation because the local rule reads \(q,qa,qb\), and the matrix-coefficient calculation above gives the displayed \(\pi(a),\pi(b)\) blocks without a transpose or inverse. Under the common dual convention the blocks are contragredient. On characters this replaces \((u,v)\) by \((u^{-1},v^{-1})\), a permutation of \(\mu_\ell(k)^2\). On nonlinear modules it replaces the central scalar \(\zeta\) by \(\zeta^{-1}\), a permutation of the nontrivial central characters. Hence the summed nullities in [\[eq:nullity-ledger\]](#eq:nullity-ledger) are unchanged even though individual matrices need not be identical.
papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage3/ANCHORED_REVIEW_DRAFT.md:182:# Coefficient phase diagram and finite controls
papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage3/ANCHORED_REVIEW_DRAFT.md:188:\[cor:phase-diagram\] On \(\mathbb P^2(\Fp)\) away from the coordinate axes, the nonlinear part of the fixed dimension is zero off the Fermat curve \[\alpha^\ell+\beta^\ell+\gamma^\ell=0\] and equals \(\ell(\ell-1)\) on that curve. The remaining variation is the bounded term \(\Dcycl\le\ell\).
papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage3/ANCHORED_REVIEW_DRAFT.md:230:Principal algebraic actions of the discrete Heisenberg group and the exact integer element \(1+a+b\) precede this paper \[@GollSchmidtVerbitskiy2014; @LindSchmidt2015\]. Finite Heisenberg Stone–von Neumann theory and the explicit finite-group irreducible ledger are also standard \[@GurevichHadani2010; @GrassbergerHormann2001\]. Deundyak and Leonov already supply left/right convolution, noncommutative Fourier blocks, and a blockwise equation-solving algorithm on finite Heisenberg groups \[@DeundyakLeonov2016\]. We therefore claim none of those frameworks. After that owner subtraction, the residual P70 claim is the weighted cross-characteristic congruence-nullity formula: its character gcd term, the central-character-independent Fermat determinant, exact corank-one singular blocks, and the resulting \(\ell(\ell-1)\) regular-representation jump. A bounded search through 26 August 2026 found no exact match for that conjunction, but this is not a worldwide novelty or priority certificate.
papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage3/ANCHORED_REVIEW_DRAFT.md:236:The assumption \(p\ne\ell\) is structural: at \(p=\ell\), the finite regular module need not be semisimple, and nilpotent extensions can change the nullity. We also require all three coefficients to be nonzero. Degenerate two-term rules admit separate elementary case splits but are outside the selected theorem. Finally, congruence-fixed dimensions do not by themselves settle expansiveness, entropy, density of periodic points, or generator questions for the infinite action.
papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage3/ANCHORED_REVIEW_DRAFT.md:272:Weighted three-term Heisenberg shifts have an exact finite-quotient phase diagram. Character blocks contribute the cyclotomic intersection degree \(\Dcycl\), while every nonlinear block is controlled by the same Fermat polynomial \(\alpha^\ell+\beta^\ell+\gamma^\ell\). The regular multiplicities turn its vanishing into a jump of \(\ell(\ell-1)\). The unit-coefficient characteristic-three phenomenon is therefore one specialization of a coefficient-space theorem rather than an isolated determinant coincidence.

papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage2_5/draft_for_claim_registry_round1.md:8: \alpha x_g+\beta x_{ga}+\gamma x_{gb}=0.\] The underlying Heisenberg principal-action framework is established. In particular, \[@GollSchmidtVerbitskiy2014\] develop expansiveness and homoclinic methods for principal actions, and the survey of \[@LindSchmidt2015\] treats the exact integer element \(1+a+b\) as a mixing example. Our object is its zero-dimensional coefficient reduction, not a claim that the group, action framework, or three-term element is new. The question here is narrower and arithmetic: what is the exact kernel dimension after imposing a prime congruence period?
papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage2_5/draft_for_claim_registry_round1.md:22:Finite Heisenberg representation theory, including the Stone–von Neumann description behind the nonlinear blocks, is standard; we use the formulation of \[@GurevichHadani2010\] and the direct finite-group classification of \[@GrassbergerHormann2001\]. The progress here is the resulting symbolic fixed-space phase diagram for the weighted cross-characteristic family. No priority language is intended: the literature search supporting this internal draft is bounded.
papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage2_5/draft_for_claim_registry_round1.md:100:<span id="rem:left-right" label="rem:left-right">\[rem:left-right\]</span> Our finite operator is right translation because the local rule reads \(q,qa,qb\), and the matrix-coefficient calculation above gives the displayed \(\pi(a),\pi(b)\) blocks without a transpose or inverse. Under the common dual convention the blocks are contragredient. On characters this replaces \((u,v)\) by \((u^{-1},v^{-1})\), a permutation of \(\mu_\ell(k)^2\). On nonlinear modules it replaces the central scalar \(\zeta\) by \(\zeta^{-1}\), a permutation of the nontrivial central characters. Hence the summed nullities in [\[eq:nullity-ledger\]](#eq:nullity-ledger) are unchanged even though individual matrices need not be identical.
papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage2_5/draft_for_claim_registry_round1.md:138:# Coefficient phase diagram and finite controls
papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage2_5/draft_for_claim_registry_round1.md:142:<span id="cor:phase-diagram" label="cor:phase-diagram">\[cor:phase-diagram\]</span> On \(\mathbb P^2(\Fp)\) away from the coordinate axes, the nonlinear part of the fixed dimension is zero off the Fermat curve \[\alpha^\ell+\beta^\ell+\gamma^\ell=0\] and equals \(\ell(\ell-1)\) on that curve. The remaining variation is the bounded term \(\Dcycl\le\ell\).
papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage2_5/draft_for_claim_registry_round1.md:176:Principal algebraic actions of the discrete Heisenberg group and the exact integer element \(1+a+b\) precede this paper \[@GollSchmidtVerbitskiy2014; @LindSchmidt2015\]. Finite Heisenberg Stone–von Neumann theory and the explicit finite-group irreducible ledger are also standard \[@GurevichHadani2010; @GrassbergerHormann2001\]. Deundyak and Leonov already supply left/right convolution, noncommutative Fourier blocks, and a blockwise equation-solving algorithm on finite Heisenberg groups \[@DeundyakLeonov2016\]. We therefore claim none of those frameworks. After that owner subtraction, the residual P70 claim is the weighted cross-characteristic congruence-nullity formula: its character gcd term, the central-character-independent Fermat determinant, exact corank-one singular blocks, and the resulting \(\ell(\ell-1)\) regular-representation jump. A bounded search through 26 August 2026 found no exact match for that conjunction, but this is not a worldwide novelty or priority certificate.
papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage2_5/draft_for_claim_registry_round1.md:180:The assumption \(p\ne\ell\) is structural: at \(p=\ell\), the finite regular module need not be semisimple, and nilpotent extensions can change the nullity. We also require all three coefficients to be nonzero. Degenerate two-term rules admit separate elementary case splits but are outside the selected theorem. Finally, congruence-fixed dimensions do not by themselves settle expansiveness, entropy, density of periodic points, or generator questions for the infinite action.
papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage2_5/draft_for_claim_registry_round1.md:204:Weighted three-term Heisenberg shifts have an exact finite-quotient phase diagram. Character blocks contribute the cyclotomic intersection degree \(\Dcycl\), while every nonlinear block is controlled by the same Fermat polynomial \(\alpha^\ell+\beta^\ell+\gamma^\ell\). The regular multiplicities turn its vanishing into a jump of \(\ell(\ell-1)\). The unit-coefficient characteristic-three phenomenon is therefore one specialization of a coefficient-space theorem rather than an isolated determinant coincidence.

papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage2_5/draft_for_claim_registry.md:8: \alpha x_g+\beta x_{ga}+\gamma x_{gb}=0.\] The underlying Heisenberg principal-action framework is established. In particular, \[@GollSchmidtVerbitskiy2014\] develop expansiveness and homoclinic methods for principal actions, and the survey of \[@LindSchmidt2015\] treats the exact integer element \(1+a+b\) as a mixing example. Our object is its zero-dimensional coefficient reduction, not a claim that the group, action framework, or three-term element is new. The question here is narrower and arithmetic: what is the exact kernel dimension after imposing a prime congruence period?
papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage2_5/draft_for_claim_registry.md:20:Finite Heisenberg representation theory, including the Stone–von Neumann description behind the nonlinear blocks, is standard; we use the formulation of \[@GurevichHadani2010\]. papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage2_5/draft_for_claim_registry.md:98:<span id="rem:left-right" label="rem:left-right">\[rem:left-right\]</span> Our finite operator is right translation because the local rule reads \(q,qa,qb\), and the matrix-coefficient calculation above gives the displayed \(\pi(a),\pi(b)\) blocks without a transpose or inverse. Under the common dual convention the blocks are contragredient. On characters this replaces \((u,v)\) by \((u^{-1},v^{-1})\), a permutation of \(\mu_\ell(k)^2\). On nonlinear modules it replaces the central scalar \(\zeta\) by \(\zeta^{-1}\), a permutation of the nontrivial central characters. Hence the summed nullities in [\[eq:nullity-ledger\]](#eq:nullity-ledger) are unchanged even though individual matrices need not be identical.
papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage2_5/draft_for_claim_registry.md:136:# Coefficient phase diagram and finite controls
papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage2_5/draft_for_claim_registry.md:140:<span id="cor:phase-diagram" label="cor:phase-diagram">\[cor:phase-diagram\]</span> On \(\mathbb P^2(\Fp)\) away from the coordinate axes, the nonlinear part of the fixed dimension is zero off the Fermat curve \[\alpha^\ell+\beta^\ell+\gamma^\ell=0\] and equals \(\ell(\ell-1)\) on that curve. The remaining variation is the bounded term \(\Dcycl\le\ell\).
papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage2_5/draft_for_claim_registry.md:174:Principal algebraic actions of the discrete Heisenberg group and the exact integer element \(1+a+b\) precede this paper \[@GollSchmidtVerbitskiy2014; @LindSchmidt2015\]. Finite Heisenberg Stone–von Neumann theory is also standard \[@GurevichHadani2010\]. We claim only the weighted mod-\(p\) congruence-nullity formula proved here. papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage2_5/draft_for_claim_registry.md:178:The assumption \(p\ne\ell\) is structural: at \(p=\ell\), the finite regular module need not be semisimple, and nilpotent extensions can change the nullity. papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage2_5/draft_for_claim_registry.md:202:Weighted three-term Heisenberg shifts have an exact finite-quotient phase diagram. Character blocks contribute the cyclotomic intersection degree \(\Dcycl\), while every nonlinear block is controlled by the same Fermat polynomial \(\alpha^\ell+\beta^\ell+\gamma^\ell\). The regular multiplicities turn its vanishing into a jump of \(\ell(\ell-1)\). The unit-coefficient characteristic-three phenomenon is therefore one specialization of a coefficient-space theorem rather than an isolated determinant coincidence.

papers/29-functorial-chiral-counterterm-no-go/README.md:7:This paper classifies a scoped family of counterterms for the critical finite-cutoff quadratic ledger left by Paper 28.  For the marked integer-divisibility incidence realization, the only divergent term is the diagonal prime-harmonic germ.  The off-diagonal mixed Gram phase is absolutely summable.  Natural cutoff convergence does not select a unique finite part: leading-only and full-diagonal subtraction differ by an explicit, positive, summable atom-local shift, and further summable natural shifts remain admissible.

papers/39-affine-obstruction-dag-closure-certificate/LITERATURE_AUDIT.md:183:Radin Dardashti’s 2021 article is the most direct conceptual predecessor. It models a no-go result using a goal `G`, framework `F`, physical assumptions `P`, mathematical structures/tools `M`, and background assumptions `B`. It then treats denial of each component as a distinct methodological pathway. The paper explicitly warns that no-go results often reveal routes around an assumption or mathematical representation and therefore do not, without further work, close a research programme.
papers/39-affine-obstruction-dag-closure-certificate/LITERATURE_AUDIT.md:227:**Non-collision:** the certified objects are program safety or state-transition properties, not exhaustiveness of a source-derived mathematical research programme. Paper 39 may reuse the certificate/checker pattern but cannot claim to invent it.
papers/39-affine-obstruction-dag-closure-certificate/LITERATURE_AUDIT.md:235:Important full-text qualification: the project did **not** formalize 22 million separate end-to-end theorems. It formalized a reduced generating set (reported as 10,657 positive implications and 586,925 negative implications, with many negatives packed into shared `Facts` theorems), while transitivity and duality expansion to the full graph is currently performed by programs external to Lean. The authors state that an end-to-end Lean theorem could in principle be created. The separate finite-magma implication graph also retained one unresolved implication up to duality in the v2 project paper.
papers/39-affine-obstruction-dag-closure-certificate/LITERATURE_AUDIT.md:265:**Non-collision:** those certificates are state-pair functions that overapproximate a transition relation and establish temporal properties. They do not close a research-program repair registry, do not encode literature provenance, and do not classify category-changing exits.

papers/192-first-collision-hurwitz/NARRATIVE_REPORT.md:7:A minimal factorization of the long cycle is a word of (n-1) transpositions. Each transposition has a lower endpoint. Read the word from left to right, find the first adjacent equality among those lower endpoints, and perform the classical right Hurwitz move at that position. If there is no equality, stop. The local move is standard; the deterministic, state-selected scheduler is the only new object under analysis.
papers/192-first-collision-hurwitz/NARRATIVE_REPORT.md:55:The Python verifier exhausts (2\le n\le8); an independent C++ program streams every Prüfer word at (n=9). These are exact finite checks. They do not furnish the missing all-(n) bijection. In particular, the ordinary factorization-tree correspondence does not automatically identify scheduler execution positions with occurrences of a distinguished Prüfer symbol. The manuscript therefore labels the formula, its binomial depth sum, and the general unique-deepest-state consequence as conjectural.

papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage4/REVISED_DRAFT.md:12: \alpha x_g+\beta x_{ga}+\gamma x_{gb}=0.\] The underlying Heisenberg principal-action framework is established. In particular, \[@GollSchmidtVerbitskiy2014\] develop expansiveness and homoclinic methods for principal actions, and the survey of \[@LindSchmidt2015\] treats the exact integer element \(1+a+b\) as a mixing example. Our object is its zero-dimensional coefficient reduction, not a claim that the group, action framework, or three-term element is new. The question here is narrower and arithmetic: what is the exact kernel dimension after imposing a prime congruence period?
papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage4/REVISED_DRAFT.md:31:Finite Heisenberg representation theory, including the Stone–von Neumann description behind the nonlinear blocks, is standard; we use the formulation of \[@GurevichHadani2010\] and the direct finite-group classification of \[@GrassbergerHormann2001\]. The progress here is the resulting symbolic fixed-space phase diagram for the weighted cross-characteristic family. No priority language is intended: the literature search supporting this internal draft is bounded.
papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage4/REVISED_DRAFT.md:130:\[rem:left-right\] Our finite operator is right translation because the local rule reads \(q,qa,qb\), and the matrix-coefficient calculation above gives the displayed \(\pi(a),\pi(b)\) blocks without a transpose or inverse. Under the common dual convention the blocks are contragredient. On characters this replaces \((u,v)\) by \((u^{-1},v^{-1})\), a permutation of \(\mu_\ell(k)^2\). On nonlinear modules it replaces the central scalar \(\zeta\) by \(\zeta^{-1}\), a permutation of the nontrivial central characters. Hence the summed nullities in [\[eq:nullity-ledger\]](#eq:nullity-ledger) are unchanged even though individual matrices need not be identical.

papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage4/REVISED_DRAFT.md:254:The assumption \(p\ne\ell\) is structural: at \(p=\ell\), the finite regular module need not be semisimple, and nilpotent extensions can change the nullity. papers/70-weighted-heisenberg-congruence-nullities/stage4/REVISED_DRAFT.md:290:Weighted three-term Heisenberg shifts have an exact finite-quotient phase diagram. Character blocks contribute the cyclotomic intersection degree \(\Dcycl\), while every nonlinear block is controlled by the same Fermat polynomial \(\alpha^\ell+\beta^\ell+\gamma^\ell\). The regular multiplicities turn its vanishing into a jump of \(\ell(\ell-1)\). The unit-coefficient characteristic-three phenomenon is therefore one specialization of a coefficient-space theorem rather than an isolated determinant coincidence.

papers/30-free-monoid-incidence-indistinguishability/README.md:7:This paper closes the nonlocal divisibility-coherence loophole left by Papers 28–29.  Positive-integer divisibility is a free commutative monoid on its prime covers.  After the cutoff, roof, Gram, and compiler decorations admitted by the project are transported, the integer source is isomorphic to a formal free-commutative/UFD clone.  Every isomorphism-natural local or nonlocal invariant of those data therefore takes the same value on the baseline and the clone.  The requested gate, nonzero on the integer baseline and zero on every UFD control, is inconsistent.
papers/30-free-monoid-incidence-indistinguishability/README.md:9:The frozen Boolean-join weight is natural only after its support and normalization are imposed.  It is identically one on squarefree Boolean tuple intervals and hence survives on the free-UFD clone.  Its partition-lattice connected cumulant instead vanishes on the baseline whenever the moment factorizes.  Embedding the pair weight into the Paper28/29 mixed Gram series gives a holomorphic new functional with an exact ratio-frequency ledger, but not an ordinary trace, a relative determinant, or a modified Fredholm determinant.  The clone carries the same ledger.
